              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Preference'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/addresses:
        get:
          tags: [App Profile]
          operationId: listAddresses
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Saved addresses
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema:
                    type: array
                    items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Address'}
        post:
          tags: [App Profile]
          operationId: createAddress
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AddressWrite'}
          responses:
            '201':
              description: Created address
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Address'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/addresses/{addressId}:
        patch:
          tags: [App Profile]
          operationId: updateAddress
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/AddressId'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AddressWrite'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Updated address
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Address'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
        delete:
          tags: [App Profile]
          operationId: deleteAddress
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/AddressId'}
          responses:
            '204': {description: Address deleted}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
    
      /api/v1/app/orders/quote:
        post:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: quoteOrder
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderRequest'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Authoritative quote and resolved slot
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderQuote'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/orders:
        get:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: listOwnOrders
          parameters:
            - name: status
              in: query
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Page'}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Size'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Own order page
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderPage'}
        post:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: placeOrder
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/IdempotencyKey'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderRequest'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Replay of an already completed request
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '201':
              description: MySQL-confirmed order
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '202':
              description: Reservation accepted but MySQL confirmation is pending
              headers:
                Retry-After: {schema: {type: integer, minimum: 1}}
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderSubmission'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
            '422': {$ref: '#/components/responses/BusinessProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/order-submissions/{submissionId}:
        get:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: getOrderSubmission
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/SubmissionId'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Processing result
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderSubmission'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/orders/{orderId}:
        get:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: getOwnOrder
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/OrderId'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Own order details
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
      /api/v1/app/orders/{orderId}/cancel:
        post:
          tags: [App Orders]
          operationId: cancelOwnOrder
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/OrderId'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/VersionRequest'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Cancelled order
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
    
      /api/v1/admin/dishes:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminListDishes
          parameters:
            - name: q
              in: query
              schema: {type: string, maxLength: 50}
            - name: category
              in: query
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Category'}
            - name: published
              in: query
              schema: {type: boolean}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Page'}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Size'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Dish page
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDishPage'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
        post:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminCreateDish
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/DishWrite'}
          responses:
            '201':
              description: Created dish
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDish'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/dishes/{dishId}:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminGetDish
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/DishId'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Admin dish model
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDish'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
        put:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminUpdateDish
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/DishId'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/DishWrite'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Updated dish
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDish'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/dishes/{dishId}/publication:
        patch:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminSetDishPublication
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/DishId'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/PublicationRequest'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Updated dish
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDish'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/media/images:
        post:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminUploadImage
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              multipart/form-data:
                schema:
                  type: object
                  required: [file]
                  properties:
                    file: {type: string, format: binary}
          responses:
            '201':
              description: Stored media URL
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/MediaUpload'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/menus/{date}:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminGetDailyMenu
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/PathDate'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Menu editor model
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDailyMenu'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
        put:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminUpsertDailyMenu
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/PathDate'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/DailyMenuWrite'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Updated menu
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDailyMenu'}
            '400': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ValidationProblem'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/menus/{date}/publish:
        post:
          tags: [Admin Catalog]
          operationId: adminPublishDailyMenu
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/PathDate'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Published menu
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AdminDailyMenu'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
    
      /api/v1/admin/orders:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Orders]
          operationId: adminListOrders
          parameters:
            - name: q
              in: query
              schema: {type: string, maxLength: 50}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Date'}
            - name: mealSlot
              in: query
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/MealSlot'}
            - name: status
              in: query
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Page'}
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Size'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: All-order page
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderPage'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/orders/{orderId}:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Orders]
          operationId: adminGetOrder
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/OrderId'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Order with history
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '404': {$ref: '#/components/responses/NotFoundProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/orders/{orderId}/transitions:
        post:
          tags: [Admin Orders]
          operationId: adminTransitionOrder
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/OrderId'}
          requestBody:
            required: true
            content:
              application/json:
                schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderTransitionRequest'}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Transitioned order
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Order'}
            '409': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ConflictProblem'}
      /api/v1/admin/dashboard:
        get:
          tags: [Admin Dashboard]
          operationId: adminGetDashboard
          parameters:
            - {$ref: '#/components/parameters/Date'}
            - name: refresh
              in: query
              schema: {type: boolean, default: false}
          responses:
            '200':
              description: Dashboard snapshot from real orders
              content:
                application/json:
                  schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Dashboard'}
            '403': {$ref: '#/components/responses/ForbiddenProblem'}
    
    components:
      securitySchemes:
        bearerAuth:
          type: http
          scheme: bearer
          bearerFormat: JWT
      security:
        - bearerAuth: []
      parameters:
        Date:
          name: date
          in: query
          required: false
          schema: {type: string, format: date}
        PathDate:
          name: date
          in: path
          required: true
          schema: {type: string, format: date}
        Page:
          name: page
          in: query
          schema: {type: integer, minimum: 0, default: 0}
        Size:
          name: size
          in: query
          schema: {type: integer, minimum: 1, maximum: 100, default: 20}
        DishId:
          name: dishId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 1}
        AddressId:
          name: addressId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 1}
        OrderId:
          name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 1}
        SubmissionId:
          name: submissionId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema: {type: string, format: uuid}
        IdempotencyKey:
          name: Idempotency-Key
          in: header
          required: true
          schema: {type: string, format: uuid}
      responses:
        TokenResponse:
          description: Access token response; also sets the rotating `webox_refresh` cookie.
          headers:
            Set-Cookie:
              schema: {type: string}
              description: webox_refresh; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/api/v1/auth; Max-Age=1209600; Secure outside local profile
          content:
            application/json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/TokenResponse'}
        ValidationProblem:
          description: Invalid request
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        AuthProblem:
          description: Authentication required or invalid credentials
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        ForbiddenProblem:
          description: Authenticated actor is not permitted
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        NotFoundProblem:
          description: Resource not found
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        ConflictProblem:
          description: State, idempotency, active-order, or inventory conflict
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        BusinessProblem:
          description: Business precondition requires user review
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
        RateLimitProblem:
          description: Rate limited
          headers:
            Retry-After: {schema: {type: integer, minimum: 1}}
          content:
            application/problem+json:
              schema: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Problem'}
      schemas:
        Role:
          type: string
          enum: [EMPLOYEE, ADMIN]
        Category:
          type: string
          enum: [CHINESE, WESTERN, JAPANESE, LIGHT_MEAL, KOREAN, SOUTHEAST_ASIAN]
        SpiceLevel:
          type: string
          enum: [NONE, MILD, MEDIUM, HOT]
        Allergen:
          type: string
          enum: [PEANUTS, DAIRY, EGG, GLUTEN, SOY, FISH, SHELLFISH]
        TasteIntensity:
          type: string
          enum: [LIGHT, BALANCED, RICH]
        MealSlot:
          type: string
          enum: [LUNCH, DINNER]
        OrderStatus:
          type: string
          enum: [PENDING, CONFIRMED, COMPLETED, CANCELLED]
        Money:
          type: string
          pattern: '^\d{1,8}\.\d{2}$'
          examples: ['22.50']
        User:
          type: object
          required: [id, email, displayName, role]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            email: {type: string, format: email}
            displayName: {type: string}
            role: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Role'}
        RegisterRequest:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [email, password, displayName]
          properties:
            email: {type: string, format: email, maxLength: 200}
            password: {type: string, minLength: 8, maxLength: 72, pattern: '^(?=.*[A-Za-z])(?=.*\d).+$'}
            displayName: {type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 100}
        LoginRequest:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [email, password]
          properties:
            email: {type: string, format: email, maxLength: 200}
            password: {type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 72}
        TokenResponse:
          type: object
          required: [accessToken, tokenType, expiresIn, user]
          properties:
            accessToken: {type: string}
            tokenType: {type: string, const: Bearer}
            expiresIn: {type: integer, const: 900}
            user: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/User'}
        FieldError:
          type: object
          required: [field, message]
          properties:
            field: {type: string}
            message: {type: string}
        Problem:
          type: object
          required: [type, title, status, detail, code, correlationId]
          properties:
            type: {type: string, format: uri}
            title: {type: string}
            status: {type: integer}
            detail: {type: string}
            code: {type: string}
            correlationId: {type: string}
            fieldErrors:
              type: array
              items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/FieldError'}
            details: {type: object, additionalProperties: true}
        PageMeta:
          type: object
          required: [page, size, totalItems, totalPages]
          properties:
            page: {type: integer, minimum: 0}
            size: {type: integer, minimum: 1}
            totalItems: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 0}
            totalPages: {type: integer, minimum: 0}
        OptionValue:
          type: object
          required: [id, name, priceDelta]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            name: {type: string}
            priceDelta: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
        OptionGroup:
          type: object
          required: [id, name, selectionType, required, minSelections, maxSelections, values]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            name: {type: string}
            selectionType: {type: string, enum: [SINGLE, MULTIPLE]}
            required: {type: boolean}
            minSelections: {type: integer, minimum: 0}
            maxSelections: {type: integer, minimum: 1}
            values: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OptionValue'}}
        DishCard:
          type: object
          required: [menuItemId, dishId, name, description, imageUrl, basePrice, category, allergens, spiceLevel, remainingStock, stockVersion, lowStock, soldOut, preferenceMatches]
          properties:
            menuItemId: {type: integer, format: int64}
            dishId: {type: integer, format: int64}
            name: {type: string}
            description: {type: string}
            imageUrl: {type: string}
            basePrice: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            category: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Category'}
            allergens: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Allergen'}}
            spiceLevel: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/SpiceLevel'}
            remainingStock: {type: integer, minimum: 0}
            stockVersion: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 0}
            lowStock: {type: boolean}
            soldOut: {type: boolean}
            preferenceMatches: {type: array, items: {type: string}}
        DishDetail:
          allOf:
            - {$ref: '#/components/schemas/DishCard'}
            - type: object
              required: [proteinSource, optionGroups]
              properties:
                proteinSource: {type: string}
                optionGroups: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OptionGroup'}}
        MenuPage:
          allOf:
            - {$ref: '#/components/schemas/PageMeta'}
            - type: object
              required: [date, items]
              properties:
                date: {type: string, format: date}
                items: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/DishCard'}}
        Preference:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [allergens, preferredCategories]
          properties:
            allergens: {type: array, uniqueItems: true, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Allergen'}}
            preferredCategories: {type: array, uniqueItems: true, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Category'}}
            spiceLevel: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/SpiceLevel'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            tasteIntensity: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/TasteIntensity'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            budgetMin: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            budgetMax: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}, {type: 'null'}]}
        Address:
          type: object
          required: [id, label, addressText, default]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            label: {type: string}
            addressText: {type: string}
            default: {type: boolean}
        AddressWrite:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [label, addressText, default]
          properties:
            label: {type: string, maxLength: 50}
            addressText: {type: string, minLength: 1, maxLength: 200}
            default: {type: boolean}
        OrderLineRequest:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [dishId, quantity, optionValueIds]
          properties:
            dishId: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 1}
            quantity: {type: integer, minimum: 1, maximum: 5}
            optionValueIds: {type: array, uniqueItems: true, items: {type: integer, format: int64, minimum: 1}}
        InlineAddress:
          allOf:
            - {$ref: '#/components/schemas/AddressWrite'}
            - type: object
              required: [save]
              properties:
                save: {type: boolean}
        OrderRequest:
          type: object
          additionalProperties: false
          required: [deliveryDate, mealSlot, items]
          properties:
            deliveryDate: {type: string, format: date}
            mealSlot: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/MealSlot'}
            addressId: {type: [integer, 'null'], format: int64, minimum: 1}
            newAddress: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/InlineAddress'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            acceptedResolvedDeliveryDate: {type: [string, 'null'], format: date}
            acceptedResolvedMealSlot: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/MealSlot'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            items:
              type: array
              minItems: 1
              maxItems: 5
              items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderLineRequest'}
          description: Exactly one of addressId/newAddress is required. Sum of item quantities must be at most 5.
        QuoteLine:
          type: object
          required: [dishId, dishName, quantity, optionValueIds, unitPrice, subtotal]
          properties:
            dishId: {type: integer, format: int64}
            dishName: {type: string}
            quantity: {type: integer}
            optionValueIds: {type: array, items: {type: integer, format: int64}}
            unitPrice: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            subtotal: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
        ExistingOrder:
          type: object
          required: [id, orderNumber, status]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            orderNumber: {type: string}
            status: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'}
        OrderQuote:
          type: object
          required: [resolvedDeliveryDate, resolvedMealSlot, slotAdjusted, lines, total, warnings, unavailableItems]
          properties:
            resolvedDeliveryDate: {type: string, format: date}
            resolvedMealSlot: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/MealSlot'}
            slotAdjusted: {type: boolean}
            lines: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/QuoteLine'}}
            total: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            warnings: {type: array, items: {type: string, enum: [BUDGET_EXCEEDED, SLOT_ADJUSTED]}}
            unavailableItems: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/InventoryConflictItem'}}
            existingOrder: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/ExistingOrder'}, {type: 'null'}]}
        InventoryConflictItem:
          type: object
          required: [dishId, name, requested, remaining]
          properties:
            dishId: {type: integer, format: int64}
            name: {type: string}
            requested: {type: integer}
            remaining: {type: integer}
        OrderOption:
          type: object
          required: [groupName, valueName, priceDelta]
          properties:
            groupName: {type: string}
            valueName: {type: string}
            priceDelta: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
        OrderItem:
          type: object
          required: [id, dishId, dishName, basePrice, unitPrice, quantity, subtotal, options]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            dishId: {type: integer, format: int64}
            dishName: {type: string}
            basePrice: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            unitPrice: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            quantity: {type: integer}
            subtotal: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Money'}
            options: {type: array, items: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderOption'}}
        StatusHistory:
          type: object
          required: [fromStatus, toStatus, actorRole, actorDisplayName, createdAt]
          properties:
            fromStatus: {anyOf: [{$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'}, {type: 'null'}]}
            toStatus: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderStatus'}
            actorRole: {$ref: '#/components/schemas/Role'}
            actorDisplayName: {type: string}
            reason: {type: [string, 'null']}
            createdAt: {type: string, format: date-time}
        Order:
          type: object
          required: [id, orderNumber, userId, userDisplayName, deliveryDate, mealSlot, deliveryAddress, status, total, version, items, statusHistory, createdAt]
          properties:
            id: {type: integer, format: int64}
            orderNumber: {type: string}

    # WeBox design documentation
    
    This directory is the review package for the proposed WeBox implementation. It is a design-only deliverable: no application, database, or infrastructure has been initialized yet.
    
    ## Recommended reading order
    
    1. [Requirements and scope](requirements-and-scope.md) — requirement traceability, assumptions, and exclusions
    2. [System architecture](architecture.md) — architecture choice, modules, data flow, reliability, and AI extension seam
    3. [Backend design](backend-design.md) — Spring Boot structure, concurrency, caching, security, and tests
    4. [Frontend design](frontend-design.md) — React suitability, application structure, responsive UX, and tests
    5. [API contract](api.md) — proposed HTTP/SSE endpoints and error conventions
    6. [Database schema](database/schema.sql) — executable MySQL 8 DDL
    7. [Seed data plan](database/seed-data.md) — English demo data and product-image mapping
    8. [Local initialization and delivery](setup-and-delivery.md) — proposed prerequisites, configuration, startup, and delivery checklist
    9. [Development plan](development-plan.md) — phased implementation and acceptance gates
    10. [Appendix A score assessment](appendix-a-score-assessment.md) — projected score, evidence gates, and deduction risks
    11. [Decisions for confirmation](decisions-for-confirmation.md) — assumptions and resolved review decisions
    
    ## Decision summary
    
    - **Architecture:** modular monolith, not microservices. It preserves clear domain boundaries without creating distributed-system overhead for roughly 200 employees.
    - **Frontend:** React + TypeScript is suitable. It supports a responsive employee SPA and data-heavy Console with one component model and a mature testing ecosystem.
    - **Backend:** Java 17 + Spring Boot, package-by-feature, with application/domain/infrastructure boundaries inside each feature.
    - **Persistence:** MySQL 8 is the system of record. Money is stored as `DECIMAL(10,2)` and calculated with Java `BigDecimal`.
    - **API boundaries:** employee APIs use `/api/v1/app/**`, Console APIs use `/api/v1/admin/**`, and authentication uses `/api/v1/auth/**`.
    - **Authentication:** short-lived access JWT in the `Authorization` header plus a rotating long-lived refresh token in a secure `HttpOnly` cookie.
    - **Redis:** hot-menu cache, rate limiting, refresh-token state, atomic inventory reservation, Redis Streams, and stock event fan-out.
    - **Inventory:** Redis Lua serializes hot-stock reservations and appends a Stream event atomically. An asynchronous idempotent consumer confirms the change in MySQL; no order is reported successful before MySQL confirmation.
    - **Real time:** Server-Sent Events (SSE) push stock changes; reconnect/focus revalidation makes event loss self-healing.
    - **AI readiness:** a provider-neutral recommendation port and SSE contract are reserved, but Tier 3 AI is not in the requested implementation scope.
    - **Console orders:** no message queue is required for operator actions, but the Admin API implements and audits the complete `Pending → Confirmed → Completed` / cancellation state machine.
    - **User-facing language:** English only. Internal code and documentation may use English; this review explanation is in Chinese.
    
    ✓ • 0ms

    # API contract execution decisions
    
    `docs/openapi.yaml` is the binding frontend/backend contract. `docs/api.md` explains intent; if wording differs, OpenAPI field names and shapes win until both documents are deliberately revised together.
    
    ## Completeness gate
    
    | Boundary | Binding decision |
    |---|---|
    | Namespaces | Employee `/api/v1/app/**`; Console `/api/v1/admin/**`; tokens `/api/v1/auth/**` |
    | Auth transport | `Authorization: Bearer <JWT>`; 15-minute access token in memory only |
    | Refresh | `webox_refresh` opaque rotating cookie; 14 days; `HttpOnly`; `SameSite=Strict`; `Path=/api/v1/auth`; `Secure` except local |
    | Local origins | `http://localhost:5173`; credentials allowed only for refresh/logout; allowed headers include `Authorization`, `Content-Type`, `Idempotency-Key`, `X-Correlation-Id` |
    | Cookie CSRF | Refresh/logout reject missing or non-allowlisted `Origin`; state-changing App/Admin APIs do not authenticate by cookie |
    | Actor | JWT filter creates user ID and role; client-supplied user/role fields are never accepted |
    | Money | JSON string with exactly two decimals; frontend converts to integer fen for arithmetic; backend uses `BigDecimal` |
    | Time | Date `YYYY-MM-DD`; instant ISO-8601 with offset; business clock `Asia/Shanghai` |
    | Pagination | Zero-based `page`; default 20; maximum 100; response carries `page,size,totalItems,totalPages,items` |
    | Errors | `application/problem+json` with `code`, English `detail`, `correlationId`, optional `fieldErrors/details` |
    | Order request | Exactly one of `addressId/newAddress`; total quantity ≤ 5; server reprices all lines |
    | Cutoff change | Quote returns resolved date/slot; placement must echo accepted values or receives `422 ORDER_SLOT_ADJUSTED` |
    | Idempotency | UUID `Idempotency-Key`; same hash replays; different hash returns `409 IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` |
    | Async inventory | `201` means MySQL-confirmed; `202` means keep polling; UI cannot show success or clear cart on `202` |
    | State machine | Employee cancels Pending; Admin confirms Pending, completes Confirmed, or cancels Pending/Confirmed with expected version |
    | Stock SSE | Fetch-based SSE with bearer header; `stock.changed` payload contains date/menuItemId/remainingStock/stockVersion |
    | Dashboard | Delivery-date based; total includes cancelled; revenue/rank/distribution exclude cancelled; trend has seven dates |
    | AI | Provider seam only; no live AI endpoint is required in this implementation |
    
    ## Local integration endpoints
    
    ```text
    Frontend: http://localhost:5173
    Backend:  http://localhost:8080
    MySQL:    127.0.0.1:3306 / root / empty password / database webox
    Redis:    127.0.0.1:6379 / no authentication
    ```
    
    The verified host currently has JDK 17.0.12 installed (although JDK 21 is the shell default), MySQL responds as `root@localhost`, and Redis responds with `PONG`. Backend commands must set `JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17)` so compilation proves Java 17 compatibility.
    
    ## Parallel ownership
    
    - Frontend agent writes only `frontend/` and its report file.
    - Backend agent writes only `backend/`, runtime media copied under `backend/`, and its report file.
    - Neither agent changes `docs/openapi.yaml` or shared design documents. Contract ambiguities are reported to the coordinator rather than resolved unilaterally.
    - The coordinator owns cross-stack verification, contract adjudication, shared documentation, and final acceptance.

    # WeBox Backend Implementation Plan
    
    > **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: use `superpowers:test-driven-development` and execute every behavioral step red → green → refactor. Do not spawn additional agents.
    
    **Goal:** Build the Java 17 Spring Boot modular monolith in `backend/`, implementing the binding OpenAPI contract with MySQL/Redis integration.
    
    **Architecture:** Package-by-feature modular monolith. MySQL is the durable authority; Redis provides refresh state, caching, limits, atomic stock reservation, Streams, and SSE fan-out. A centralized Spring Security JWT filter establishes `AuthenticatedActor`, while URI and method/domain checks enforce authorization.
    
    **Tech Stack:** Java 17, Spring Boot, Spring MVC/Security/Validation/Data JPA/Data Redis, Flyway, MySQL, Redis/Lettuce, Nimbus/Jose JWT support, springdoc, JUnit 5, AssertJ, MockMvc, ArchUnit.
    
    **Spec:** `docs/backend-design.md`, `docs/database/schema.sql`, `docs/database/seed.sql`, `docs/openapi.yaml`, and `docs/api-contract-decisions.md`
    
    ## Global Constraints
    
    - Write only under `backend/` and `.superpowers/reports/backend-report.md`; never edit `frontend/` or `docs/`.
    - Compile and test with `JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17)` and Maven Wrapper.
    - Local integration endpoints are MySQL `root`/empty password at `127.0.0.1:3306` and Redis/no auth at `127.0.0.1:6379`.
    - Use a dedicated `webox_test` schema for destructive integration setup; never clean/drop an unknown schema.
    - Treat `docs/openapi.yaml` as binding. All response messages rendered to users are English.
    - Every behavior starts with a focused failing test and observed expected failure. Do not add production behavior before its red test.
    - Do not commit while the frontend agent is working in the same repository.
    
    ## Planned file structure
    
    ```text
    backend/
    ├── pom.xml
    ├── mvnw
    ├── src/main/java/com/webox/
    │   ├── shared/
    │   ├── identity/{api,application,domain,infrastructure}/
    │   ├── catalog/{api,application,domain,infrastructure}/
    │   ├── menu/
    │   ├── preference/
    │   ├── ordering/
    │   ├── inventory/
    │   ├── reporting/
    │   └── platform/{security,redis,outbox,config}/
    ├── src/main/resources/{application.yml,application-local.yml,db/migration/}
    └── src/test/java/com/webox/
    ```
    
    ### Task 1: Build, configuration, Flyway schema, and English seeds
    
    **Files:** create Maven project/config, versioned migrations, application profiles, health endpoint tests.
    
    **Produces:** Java 17 build, `webox` local configuration, `webox_test` integration profile, schema validation, today's/tomorrow's published menu, and local demo-user bootstrap from environment passwords.
    
    - [ ] Write failing context/integration tests that require MySQL/Redis connectivity, Flyway tables, today/tomorrow menu rows, and no plaintext password.
    - [ ] Run the focused tests with JDK 17 and observe expected missing-schema/config failures.
    - [ ] Add minimal build/config/migrations copied and versioned from the reviewed DDL; copy visually selected seed images into backend resources/uploads.
    - [ ] Re-run focused tests and `./mvnw -q -DskipTests package`.
    
    ### Task 2: JWT authentication, rotating refresh cookie, and unified actor authorization
    
    **Files:** create `identity/*`, `platform/security/*`, security tests.
    
    **Produces:** register/login/refresh/logout/me, BCrypt, 15-minute JWT, 14-day rotating refresh family in Redis, reuse detection, `JwtAuthenticationFilter`, `AuthenticatedActor`, URI roles.
    
    - [ ] Write failing unit/MockMvc/integration tests for password rules, duplicate email, generic login error, token claims/expiry, refresh rotation/reuse revocation, Origin checks, employee Admin denial, expired JWT, and explicit actor propagation.
    - [ ] Observe red failures for missing behavior.
    - [ ] Implement the minimum security flow. Store only refresh-token hashes and never accept algorithm/role/user from request fields.
    - [ ] Run security tests and full tests; inspect logs to ensure tokens/passwords are absent.
    
    ### Task 3: Catalog, daily menus, preferences, addresses, media, and caching
    
    **Files:** create feature packages, repositories/mappers/controllers, tests.
    
    **Produces:** App menu/detail/filter/search/pagination, preference/address APIs, Admin dish/options/image/publication and daily-menu APIs, cache-aside invalidation.
    
    - [ ] Write failing domain/API/integration tests for English DTOs, name/description search, enum/length limits, unpublished visibility, option constraints, ownership, image MIME/size checks, optimistic versions, menu stock edit invariants, cache hit/invalidation/fallback.
    - [ ] Observe expected failures.
    - [ ] Implement minimal domain and adapters; use parameter binding and sort allowlists.
    - [ ] Run feature tests, MySQL/Redis integration tests, and full suite.
    
    ### Task 4: Quote, cutoff, idempotency, and order persistence

    # WeBox Frontend Implementation Plan
    
    > **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: use `superpowers:test-driven-development` and execute every behavioral step red → green → refactor. Do not spawn additional agents.
    
    **Goal:** Build the complete responsive employee SPA and Admin Console in `frontend/`, integrated against the binding OpenAPI contract.
    
    **Architecture:** React + strict TypeScript + Vite. TanStack Query owns server state, Zustand owns user-scoped cart state, React Hook Form/Zod own input validation, Ant Design supplies accessible primitives, and ECharts renders the dashboard. All API access passes through one bearer-token client with single-flight refresh.
    
    **Tech Stack:** React, TypeScript, Vite, React Router, TanStack Query, Zustand, React Hook Form, Zod, Ant Design, ECharts, Vitest, React Testing Library, MSW, Playwright, axe.
    
    **Spec:** `docs/frontend-design.md`, `docs/openapi.yaml`, and `docs/api-contract-decisions.md`
    
    ## Global Constraints
    
    - Write only under `frontend/` and `.superpowers/reports/frontend-report.md`; never edit `backend/` or `docs/`.
    - Treat `docs/openapi.yaml` as the binding field/path contract. Report ambiguity instead of changing the contract.
    - All user-visible strings, seeded mock fixtures, errors, chart labels, and accessibility labels are English.
    - Access JWT is memory-only. Refresh uses credentials and the HttpOnly cookie; never use local/session storage for tokens.
    - Currency arithmetic uses integer fen at the API boundary; render `¥` with exactly two decimals.
    - Every behavior starts with a focused failing test whose failure is observed and recorded in the report.
    - Do not commit while the backend agent is working in the same repository.
    
    ## Planned file structure
    
    ```text
    frontend/
    ├── package.json
    ├── vite.config.ts
    ├── playwright.config.ts
    ├── src/
    │   ├── app/{App,router,providers}.tsx
    │   ├── api/{client,auth,types,sse}.ts
    │   ├── shared/{money,date,problem}.ts
    │   ├── layouts/{EmployeeLayout,AdminLayout}.tsx
    │   ├── features/{auth,menu,cart,preferences,checkout,orders}/
    │   ├── features/admin/{catalog,menus,orders,dashboard}/
    │   └── test/{server,handlers,fixtures}.ts
    └── e2e/
    ```

    ---
    name: requesting-code-review
    description: Use when completing tasks, implementing major features, or before merging to verify work meets requirements
    ---
    
    # Requesting Code Review
    
    Dispatch a code reviewer subagent to catch issues before they cascade. The reviewer gets precisely crafted context for evaluation — never your session's history.
    
    **Core principle:** Review early, review often.
    
    ## When to Request Review
    
    **Mandatory:**
    - After each task in subagent-driven development
    - After completing major feature
    - Before merge to main
    
    **Optional but valuable:**
    - When stuck (fresh perspective)
    - Before refactoring (baseline check)
    - After fixing complex bug
    
    ## How to Request
    
    **1. Get git SHAs:**
    ```bash
    BASE_SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD~1)  # or origin/main
    HEAD_SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
    ```
    
    **2. Dispatch code reviewer subagent:**
    
    Dispatch a `general-purpose` subagent, filling the template at [code-reviewer.md](code-reviewer.md)
    
    **Placeholders:**
    - `{DESCRIPTION}` - Brief summary of what you built
    - `{PLAN_OR_REQUIREMENTS}` - What it should do
    - `{BASE_SHA}` - Starting commit
    - `{HEAD_SHA}` - Ending commit
    
    **3. Act on feedback:**
    - Fix Critical issues immediately
    - Fix Important issues before proceeding
    - Note Minor issues for later
    - Push back if reviewer is wrong (with reasoning)
    
    ## Example
    
    ```
    [Just completed Task 2: Add verification function]
    
    You: Let me request code review before proceeding.
    
    BASE_SHA=$(git log --oneline | grep "Task 1" | head -1 | awk '{print $1}')
    HEAD_SHA=$(git rev-parse HEAD)
    
    [Dispatch code reviewer subagent]
      DESCRIPTION: Added verifyIndex() and repairIndex() with 4 issue types
      PLAN_OR_REQUIREMENTS: Task 2 from docs/superpowers/plans/deployment-plan.md
      BASE_SHA: a7981ec
      HEAD_SHA: 3df7661
    
    [Subagent returns]:
      Strengths: Clean architecture, real tests
      Issues:
        Important: Missing progress indicators
        Minor: Magic number (100) for reporting interval
      Assessment: Ready to proceed
    
    You: [Fix progress indicators]
    [Continue to Task 3]
    ```
    
    ## Common Rationalizations
    
    | Excuse | Reality |
    |--------|---------|
    | "I'll just review the diff myself instead of dispatching a reviewer" | You're the coordinator — reviewing the diff inline burns the context window you need to keep driving the work. Dispatch a reviewer subagent: the diff and the evaluation live in its context, and only the findings come back to you. |
    | "The reviewer needs my whole session history to understand the change" | Hand it precisely crafted context, never your session's history. That keeps the reviewer on the work product, not your thought process. |
    
    ## Red Flags
    
    **Never:**
    - Skip review because "it's simple"
    - Ignore Critical issues
    - Proceed with unfixed Important issues
    - Argue with valid technical feedback
    
    **If reviewer wrong:**
    - Push back with technical reasoning
    - Show code/tests that prove it works
    - Request clarification
    
    See template at: [code-reviewer.md](code-reviewer.md)
    ---
    name: verification-before-completion
    description: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always
    ---
    
    # Verification Before Completion
    
    ## Overview
    
    **Core principle:** Evidence before claims, always.
    
    **Violating the letter of this rule is violating the spirit of this rule.**
    
    ## The Iron Law
    
    ```
    NO COMPLETION CLAIMS WITHOUT FRESH VERIFICATION EVIDENCE
    ```
    
    If you haven't run the verification command in this message, you cannot claim it passes.
    
    ## The Gate Function
    
    ```
    BEFORE claiming any status or expressing satisfaction:
    
    1. IDENTIFY: What command proves this claim?
    2. RUN: Execute the FULL command (fresh, complete)
    3. READ: Full output, check exit code, count failures
    4. VERIFY: Does output confirm the claim?
       - If NO: State actual status with evidence
       - If YES: State claim WITH evidence
    5. ONLY THEN: Make the claim
    
    Skip any step = lying, not verifying
    ```
    
    ## Common Failures
    
    | Claim | Requires | Not Sufficient |
    |-------|----------|----------------|
    | Tests pass | Test command output: 0 failures | Previous run, "should pass" |
    | Linter clean | Linter output: 0 errors | Partial check, extrapolation |
    | Build succeeds | Build command: exit 0 | Linter passing, logs look good |
    | Bug fixed | Test original symptom: passes | Code changed, assumed fixed |
    | Regression test works | Red-green cycle verified | Test passes once |
    | Agent completed | VCS diff shows changes | Agent reports "success" |
    | Requirements met | Line-by-line checklist | Tests passing |
    
    ## Red Flags - STOP
    
    - Using "should", "probably", "seems to"
    - Expressing satisfaction before verification ("Great!", "Perfect!", "Done!", etc.)
    - About to commit/push/PR without verification
    - Trusting agent success reports
    - Relying on partial verification
    - Thinking "just this once"
    - Tired and wanting work over
    - **ANY wording implying success without having run verification**
    
    ## Rationalization Prevention
    
    | Excuse | Reality |
    |--------|---------|
    | "Should work now" | RUN the verification |
    | "I'm confident" | Confidence ≠ evidence |
    | "Just this once" | No exceptions |
    | "Linter passed" | Linter ≠ compiler |
    | "Agent said success" | Verify independently |
    | "I'm tired" | Exhaustion ≠ excuse |
    | "Partial check is enough" | Partial proves nothing |
    | "Different words so rule doesn't apply" | Spirit over letter |
    
    ## Key Patterns
    
    **Tests:**
    ```
    ✅ [Run test command] [See: 34/34 pass] "All tests pass"
    ❌ "Should pass now" / "Looks correct"
    ```
    
    **Regression tests (TDD Red-Green):**
    ```
    ✅ Write → Run (pass) → Revert fix → Run (MUST FAIL) → Restore → Run (pass)
    ❌ "I've written a regression test" (without red-green verification)
    ```
    
    **Build:**
    ```
    ✅ [Run build] [See: exit 0] "Build passes"
    ❌ "Linter passed" (linter doesn't check compilation)
    ```
    
    **Requirements:**
    ```
    ✅ Re-read plan → Create checklist → Verify each → Report gaps or completion
    ❌ "Tests pass, phase complete"
    ```
    
    **Agent delegation:**
    ```
    ✅ Agent reports success → Check VCS diff → Verify changes → Report actual state
    ❌ Trust agent report
    ```
    
    ## When To Apply
    
    **ALWAYS before:**
    - ANY variation of success/completion claims
    - ANY expression of satisfaction
    - ANY positive statement about work state
    - Committing, PR creation, task completion
    - Moving to next task
    - Delegating to agents
    
    **Rule applies to:**
    - Exact phrases
    - Paraphrases and synonyms
    - Implications of success
    - ANY communication suggesting completion/correctness

    - Stock SSE via Redis Pub/Sub, MySQL outbox publishing, dashboard short cache, Admin order search/detail, and dashboard overview/ranking/distribution/seven-day trend/low-stock read models.

    ```text
    JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17) ./mvnw -q verify
    exit code: 0
    ```
    
    Surefire reports: 33 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. This includes 6 auth, 9 catalog/profile, 5 order API, 2 backend context, 1 OpenAPI path, 2 architecture, and 8 ordering-domain tests. MySQL was 9.6 and Redis responded `PONG`; Flyway applied/validated both migrations on `webox_test`. No password or JWT secret was added to the repository.
    
    The suite also uses bounded `CountDownLatch`/`Future` waits for concurrent requests; it does not use timing sleeps to coordinate the stock race.
    
    ## Concerns and remaining engineering work
    
    - Order confirmation is synchronous: Redis Lua reserves stock and appends a Stream entry, then the request transaction confirms in MySQL before returning 201. There is no Stream consumer group, pending-entry recovery, compensation/reconciliation worker, or 202 path. The response does not report success before MySQL commits, but crash recovery across Redis and MySQL still needs hardening.
    - The outbox publisher is a scheduled single-process poller without `SKIP LOCKED` claiming or multi-instance deduplication. The SSE endpoint sends resync and heartbeat events and receives published stock events, but it has no integration test for a connected client receiving a live update.
    - Redis inventory hashes initialize from current MySQL stock on first access; a Redis-loss/pending-reservation reconciler is not implemented. Redis-backed login/order/general rate limits and `429 Retry-After` behavior are also not implemented.
    - Requests are manually mapped from JSON objects in several controllers; validation is behavior-focused but is not a generated/full OpenAPI schema validator. The conformance test compares all 26 registered paths, not every schema, status, and field constraint.
    - Feature persistence currently uses `JdbcTemplate` directly in API controllers instead of the layered JPA/repository adapters in `docs/backend-design.md`.
    - The seeded dishes reference `/media/seed/image_01.jpg` through `image_09.jpg`, but no source seed images were present in the checkout to copy. Those seed image URLs need assets before the menu imagery is complete.
    - Flyway emitted its support warning because the available local MySQL is 9.6 while this Flyway version reports its latest tested MySQL support as 8.1. Migrations did pass on the configured host.
    
    No changes were made outside `backend/` and this report; no commit was created.

    package com.webox.menu.api;
    
    import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
    import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
    import com.webox.shared.ApiException;
    import com.webox.platform.security.CurrentActor;

    @RestController
    @RequestMapping("/api/v1/app/menu")
    public class MenuController {
        private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
        private final CurrentActor currentActor;
        private final RedisMessageListenerContainer listeners;
        private final TaskScheduler scheduler;
        private final ObjectMapper mapper;
        private final StringRedisTemplate redis;
    
        public MenuController(JdbcTemplate jdbc, CurrentActor currentActor, RedisMessageListenerContainer listeners,
                              TaskScheduler scheduler, ObjectMapper mapper, StringRedisTemplate redis) {
            this.jdbc = jdbc; this.currentActor = currentActor; this.listeners = listeners; this.scheduler = scheduler; this.mapper = mapper; this.redis = redis;
        }
    
        @GetMapping
        public Map<String, Object> menu(@RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate date,
                                        @RequestParam(required = false) String q,
                                        @RequestParam(required = false) List<String> categories,
                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "false") boolean recommended,
                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
            LocalDate selectedDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")) : date;
            if (page < 0 || size < 1 || size > 100) throw invalid("page", "page must be non-negative and size must be between 1 and 100");
            if (q != null && q.length() > 50) throw invalid("q", "must be at most 50 characters");
            if (categories != null && categories.stream().anyMatch(c -> !List.of("CHINESE", "WESTERN", "JAPANESE", "LIGHT_MEAL", "KOREAN", "SOUTHEAST_ASIAN").contains(c)))
                throw invalid("categories", "contains an unsupported category");
            long userId = currentActor.require().userId();
            List<String> allergens = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM user_allergen WHERE user_id=?", String.class, userId);
            List<String> preferred = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT category FROM user_preferred_category WHERE user_id=?", String.class, userId);
            Map<String, Object> preference = preferences(userId);
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = menuRows(selectedDate);
            if (q != null && !q.isBlank()) {
                String search = q.toLowerCase();
                rows.removeIf(row -> !row.get("name").toString().toLowerCase().contains(search)
                        && !row.get("description").toString().toLowerCase().contains(search));
            }
            if (categories != null && !categories.isEmpty()) rows.removeIf(row -> !categories.contains(row.get("category")));
            for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
                List<String> rowAllergens = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM dish_allergen WHERE dish_id=? ORDER BY allergen", String.class, row.get("dishId"));
                row.put("allergens", rowAllergens);
                List<String> matches = matches(row, preferred, preference);
                row.put("preferenceMatches", matches);
                row.put("allergenConflict", rowAllergens.stream().anyMatch(allergens::contains));
            }
            if (recommended) rows.removeIf(row -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(row.get("allergenConflict")));
            if (recommended) rows.sort(Comparator.<Map<String, Object>>comparingInt(row -> ((List<?>) row.get("preferenceMatches")).size()).reversed()
                    .thenComparing(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue()));
            else rows.sort(Comparator.comparing(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue()));
            long total = rows.size();
            int start = (int) Math.min(total, (long) page * size);
            int end = (int) Math.min(total, (long) start + size);
            List<Map<String, Object>> items = new ArrayList<>(rows.subList(start, end));
            for (Map<String, Object> item : items) item.remove("allergenConflict");
            Map<String, Object> result = pageMeta(page, size, total);
            result.put("date", selectedDate.toString()); result.put("items", items);
            return result;
        }
    
        @GetMapping("/dishes/{dishId}")
        public Map<String, Object> detail(@PathVariable long dishId, @RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate date) {
            LocalDate selectedDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")) : date;
            long userId = currentActor.require().userId();
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = menuRows(selectedDate).stream().filter(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue() == dishId).toList();
            if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "The dish was not found on the published menu.");
            Map<String, Object> card = rows.get(0);
            List<String> allergens = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM dish_allergen WHERE dish_id=? ORDER BY allergen", String.class, dishId);
            card.put("allergens", allergens);
            card.put("preferenceMatches", matches(card,
                    jdbc.queryForList("SELECT category FROM user_preferred_category WHERE user_id=?", String.class, userId), preferences(userId)));
            card.put("proteinSource", jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT protein_source FROM dish WHERE id=?", String.class, dishId));
            List<Map<String, Object>> groups = jdbc.query("SELECT id,name,selection_type,required_selection,min_selections,max_selections FROM dish_option_group WHERE dish_id=? AND active=TRUE ORDER BY display_order,id", (rs, n) -> {
                Map<String, Object> group = new LinkedHashMap<>();
                long id = rs.getLong("id"); group.put("id", id); group.put("name", rs.getString("name"));
                group.put("selectionType", rs.getString("selection_type")); group.put("required", rs.getBoolean("required_selection"));
                group.put("minSelections", rs.getInt("min_selections")); group.put("maxSelections", rs.getInt("max_selections"));
                group.put("values", jdbc.query("SELECT id,name,price_delta FROM dish_option_value WHERE option_group_id=? AND active=TRUE ORDER BY display_order,id", (valueRs, i) -> {
                    Map<String, Object> value = new LinkedHashMap<>(); value.put("id", valueRs.getLong("id")); value.put("name", valueRs.getString("name"));
                    value.put("priceDelta", money(valueRs.getBigDecimal("price_delta"))); return value;
                }, id));
                return group;
            }, dishId);
            card.put("optionGroups", groups);
            return card;
        }
    
        @GetMapping(path = "/stock-stream", produces = "text/event-stream")
        public SseEmitter stockStream(@RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate date) {
            LocalDate selectedDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")) : date;
            SseEmitter emitter = new SseEmitter(0L);
            ChannelTopic topic = new ChannelTopic("webox:stock:" + selectedDate);
            AtomicBoolean closed = new AtomicBoolean();
            MessageListener listener = (Message message, byte[] pattern) -> {
                if (closed.get()) return;
                try { emitter.send(SseEmitter.event().name("stock.changed").data(mapper.readTree(message.getBody()))); }
                catch (IOException ex) { emitter.completeWithError(ex); }
            };
            listeners.addMessageListener(listener, topic);
            ScheduledFuture<?> heartbeat = scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
                if (closed.get()) return;
                try { emitter.send(SseEmitter.event().name("heartbeat").data(Map.of("at", OffsetDateTime.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")).toString()))); }
                catch (IOException ex) { emitter.completeWithError(ex); }
            }, Duration.ofSeconds(20));
            Runnable cleanup = () -> {
                if (closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) { heartbeat.cancel(false); listeners.removeMessageListener(listener, topic); }
            };
            emitter.onCompletion(cleanup); emitter.onTimeout(cleanup); emitter.onError(error -> cleanup.run());
            try { emitter.send(SseEmitter.event().name("resync.required").data(Map.of("date", selectedDate.toString()))); }
            catch (IOException ex) { emitter.completeWithError(ex); }
            return emitter;
        }
    
        private List<Map<String, Object>> menuRows(LocalDate date) {
            List<Long> versions = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT publication_version FROM daily_menu WHERE menu_date=? AND status='PUBLISHED'", Long.class, date);
            if (versions.isEmpty()) return new ArrayList<>();
            String cacheKey = "webox:menu:v1:" + date + ":" + versions.get(0);
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = null;
            boolean cacheLoaded = false;
            try {
                String cached = redis.opsForValue().get(cacheKey);
                if (cached != null) { rows = mapper.readValue(cached, new TypeReference<>() { }); cacheLoaded = true; }
            } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            if (rows == null) rows = jdbc.query("SELECT mi.id AS menu_item_id,d.id AS dish_id,d.name,d.description,d.image_url,d.base_price,d.category,d.spice_level,mi.remaining_stock,mi.stock_version " +
                    "FROM daily_menu m JOIN daily_menu_item mi ON mi.daily_menu_id=m.id JOIN dish d ON d.id=mi.dish_id " +
                    "WHERE m.menu_date=? AND m.status='PUBLISHED' AND mi.visible=TRUE AND d.published=TRUE", (rs, n) -> {
                Map<String, Object> row = new LinkedHashMap<>();
                int stock = rs.getInt("remaining_stock");
                row.put("menuItemId", rs.getLong("menu_item_id")); row.put("dishId", rs.getLong("dish_id"));
                row.put("name", rs.getString("name")); row.put("description", rs.getString("description")); row.put("imageUrl", rs.getString("image_url"));
                row.put("basePrice", money(rs.getBigDecimal("base_price"))); row.put("category", rs.getString("category"));
                row.put("spiceLevel", rs.getString("spice_level")); row.put("remainingStock", stock); row.put("stockVersion", rs.getLong("stock_version"));
                row.put("lowStock", stock <= 3); row.put("soldOut", stock == 0); return row;
            }, date);
            if (!cacheLoaded) try { redis.opsForValue().set(cacheKey, mapper.writeValueAsString(rows), Duration.ofMinutes(5)); } catch (Exception ignored) { }
            for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
                Map<String, Object> stock = jdbc.queryForMap("SELECT remaining_stock,stock_version FROM daily_menu_item WHERE id=?", row.get("menuItemId"));
                int remaining = ((Number) stock.get("remaining_stock")).intValue();
                row.put("remainingStock", remaining); row.put("stockVersion", ((Number) stock.get("stock_version")).longValue());
                row.put("lowStock", remaining <= 3); row.put("soldOut", remaining == 0);
            }
            return rows;
        }
    
        private Map<String, Object> preferences(long userId) {
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT spice_level,taste_intensity,budget_min,budget_max FROM user_preference WHERE user_id=?", userId);
            if (rows.isEmpty()) return Map.of();
            Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0);
            Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("spiceLevel", row.get("spice_level")); result.put("tasteIntensity", row.get("taste_intensity"));
            result.put("budgetMin", row.get("budget_min")); result.put("budgetMax", row.get("budget_max")); return result;
        }
    
        private List<String> matches(Map<String, Object> row, List<String> preferred, Map<String, Object> preference) {
            List<String> result = new ArrayList<>();
            if (preferred.contains(row.get("category"))) result.add("PREFERRED_CATEGORY");
            if (preference.get("spiceLevel") != null && preference.get("spiceLevel").equals(row.get("spiceLevel"))) result.add("SPICE_LEVEL");
            BigDecimal price = new BigDecimal(row.get("basePrice").toString());
            Object min = preference.get("budgetMin"), max = preference.get("budgetMax");
            if ((min == null || price.compareTo(new BigDecimal(min.toString())) >= 0) && (max == null || price.compareTo(new BigDecimal(max.toString())) <= 0)
                    && (min != null || max != null)) result.add("BUDGET");
            return result;
        }
    
        private Map<String, Object> pageMeta(int page, int size, long total) {
            Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("page", page); result.put("size", size);
            result.put("totalItems", total); result.put("totalPages", total == 0 ? 0 : (int) ((total + size - 1) / size)); return result;
        }
        private String money(BigDecimal value) { return value.setScale(2).toPlainString(); }
        private ApiException invalid(String field, String detail) { return new ApiException(400, "VALIDATION_FAILED", detail, List.of(new ApiException.FieldError(field, detail))); }
    }
