       112        @GetMapping("/menus/{date}")
       113        public Map<String, Object> getMenu(@PathVariable LocalDate date) { return getDailyMenu(date); }
       114
       115        @PutMapping("/menus/{date}")
       116        @Transactional
       117        public Map<String, Object> saveMenu(@PathVariable LocalDate date, @RequestBody Map<String, Object> body) {
       118            Number expectedValue = number(body.get("expectedPublicationVersion"), "expectedPublicationVersion");
       119            long expected = expectedValue.longValue();
       120            Object rawItems = body.get("items");
       121            if (!(rawItems instanceof List<?> itemList)) throw invalid("items", "must be an array");
       122            List<MenuItemInput> inputs = new ArrayList<>();
       123            for (Object raw : itemList) {
       124                if (!(raw instanceof Map<?, ?> item)) throw invalid("items", "contains an invalid item");
       125                long dishId = number(item.get("dishId"), "dishId").longValue();
       126                int initialStock = number(item.get("initialStock"), "initialStock").intValue();
       127                if (initialStock < 0 || !(item.get("visible") instanceof Boolean visible)) throw invalid("items", "initialStock and visible are invalid");
       128                if (inputs.stream().anyMatch(existing -> existing.dishId() == dishId)) throw invalid("items", "dishIds must be unique");
       129                inputs.add(new MenuItemInput(dishId, initialStock, visible));
       130            }
       131            List<Map<String, Object>> existingMenu = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,status,publication_version FROM daily_menu WHERE menu_date=? FOR UPDATE", date);
       132            long menuId;
       133            long currentVersion;
       134            String status;
       135            if (existingMenu.isEmpty()) {
       136                if (expected != 0) throw conflict("The menu publication version has changed.");
       137                jdbc.update("INSERT INTO daily_menu(menu_date,status,publication_version) VALUES(?,'DRAFT',1)", date);
       138                menuId = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()", Long.class); currentVersion = 1; status = "DRAFT";
       139            } else {
       140                Map<String, Object> current = existingMenu.get(0);
       141                menuId = ((Number) current.get("id")).longValue(); currentVersion = ((Number) current.get("publication_version")).longValue(); status = current.get("status").toString();
       142                if (currentVersion != expected) throw conflict("The menu publication version has changed.");
       143                jdbc.update("UPDATE daily_menu SET publication_version=publication_version+1 WHERE id=? AND publication_version=?", menuId, expected);
       144                currentVersion++;
       145            }
       146            Map<Long, Map<String, Object>> previous = new LinkedHashMap<>();
       147            for (Map<String, Object> row : jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,dish_id,initial_stock,remaining_stock,visible FROM daily_menu_item WHERE daily_menu_id=? FOR UPDATE", menuId))
       148                previous.put(((Number) row.get("dish_id")).longValue(), row);
       149            for (Map<String, Object> row : previous.values()) {
       150                long dishId = ((Number) row.get("dish_id")).longValue();
       151                if (inputs.stream().noneMatch(item -> item.dishId() == dishId)) {
       152                    if (((Number) row.get("initial_stock")).intValue() != ((Number) row.get("remaining_stock")).intValue()) throw conflict("A menu item with sold stock cannot be removed.");
       153                    jdbc.update("DELETE FROM daily_menu_item WHERE id=?", row.get("id"));
       154                }
       155            }
       156            for (MenuItemInput item : inputs) {
       157                ensurePublishedDish(item.dishId());
       158                Map<String, Object> old = previous.get(item.dishId());
       159                if (old == null) jdbc.update("INSERT INTO daily_menu_item(daily_menu_id,dish_id,initial_stock,remaining_stock,visible) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)", menuId, item.dishId(), item.initialStock(), item.initialStock(), item.visible());
       160                else {
       161                    int sold = ((Number) old.get("initial_stock")).intValue() - ((Number) old.get("remaining_stock")).intValue();
       162                    if (item.initialStock() < sold) throw conflict("Initial stock cannot be lower than units already sold.");
       163                    jdbc.update("UPDATE daily_menu_item SET initial_stock=?,remaining_stock=?,visible=?,stock_version=stock_version+1 WHERE id=?",
       164                            item.initialStock(), item.initialStock() - sold, item.visible(), old.get("id"));
       165                }
       166            }
       167            return getDailyMenu(date);
       168        }
       169
       170        @PostMapping("/menus/{date}/publish")
       171        @Transactional
       172        public Map<String, Object> publishMenu(@PathVariable LocalDate date) {
       173            List<Map<String, Object>> found = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id FROM daily_menu WHERE menu_date=? FOR UPDATE", date);
       174            if (found.isEmpty()) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "The daily menu was not found.");
       175            long menuId = ((Number) found.get(0).get("id")).longValue();
       176            int count = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM daily_menu_item mi JOIN dish d ON d.id=mi.dish_id WHERE mi.daily_menu_id=? AND d.published=TRUE", Integer.class, menuId);
       177            if (count == 0) throw conflict("A menu requires at least one published dish before publication.");
       178            jdbc.update("UPDATE daily_menu SET status='PUBLISHED',published_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),publication_version=publication_version+1 WHERE id=?", menuId);
       179            return getDailyMenu(date);
       180        }
       181
       182        private Map<String, Object> getDish(long dishId) {
       183            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,name,description,base_price,category,protein_source,spice_level,image_url,published,version FROM dish WHERE id=?", dishId);
       184            if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "The dish was not found.");
       185            Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0); Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>();
       186            result.put("id", ((Number) row.get("id")).longValue()); result.put("name", row.get("name")); result.put("description", row.get("description"));
       187            result.put("basePrice", money((BigDecimal) row.get("base_price"))); result.put("category", row.get("category")); result.put("proteinSource", row.get("protein_source"));
       188            result.put("spiceLevel", row.get("spice_level")); result.put("imageUrl", row.get("image_url")); result.put("published", row.get("published")); result.put("version", ((Number) row.get("version")).longValue());
       189            long id = ((Number) row.get("id")).longValue();
       190            result.put("allergens", jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM dish_allergen WHERE dish_id=? ORDER BY allergen", String.class, id));
       191            result.put("optionGroups", optionGroups(id)); return result;
       192        }
       193
       194        private List<Map<String, Object>> optionGroups(long dishId) {
       195            return jdbc.query("SELECT id,name,selection_type,required_selection,min_selections,max_selections,display_order FROM dish_option_group WHERE dish_id=? ORDER BY display_order,id", (rs, i) -> {
       196                long id = rs.getLong("id"); Map<String, Object> group = new LinkedHashMap<>(); group.put("id", id); group.put("name", rs.getString("name"));
       197                group.put("selectionType", rs.getString("selection_type")); group.put("required", rs.getBoolean("required_selection")); group.put("minSelections", rs.getInt("min_selections"));
       198                group.put("maxSelections", rs.getInt("max_selections"));
       199                group.put("values", jdbc.query("SELECT id,name,price_delta,display_order,active FROM dish_option_value WHERE option_group_id=? ORDER BY display_order,id", (value, n) -> {
       200                    Map<String, Object> v = new LinkedHashMap<>(); v.put("id", value.getLong("id")); v.put("name", value.getString("name")); v.put("priceDelta", money(value.getBigDecimal("price_delta"))); return v;
       201                }, id)); return group;
       202            }, dishId);
       203        }
       204
       205        private DishInput dishInput(Map<String, Object> body) {
       206            String name = text(body.get("name"), "name", 1, 120); String description = text(body.get("description"), "description", 1, 1000);
       207            BigDecimal price = price(body.get("basePrice"), "basePrice"); String category = enumValue(body.get("category"), "category", List.of("CHINESE", "WESTERN", "JAPANESE", "LIGHT_MEAL", "KOREAN", "SOUTHEAST_ASIAN"));
       208            String protein = text(body.get("proteinSource"), "proteinSource", 1, 100); String spice = enumValue(body.get("spiceLevel"), "spiceLevel", List.of("NONE", "MILD", "MEDIUM", "HOT"));
       209            String imageUrl = text(body.get("imageUrl"), "imageUrl", 1, 500); boolean published = bool(body.get("published"), "published");
       210            long version = number(body.get("version"), "version").longValue();
       211            if (!(body.get("allergens") instanceof List<?> allergensRaw)) throw invalid("allergens", "must be an array");
       212            List<String> allergens = new ArrayList<>();
       213            for (Object allergen : allergensRaw) allergens.add(enumValue(allergen, "allergens", List.of("PEANUTS", "DAIRY", "EGG", "GLUTEN", "SOY", "FISH", "SHELLFISH")));
       214            if (allergens.size() != allergens.stream().distinct().count()) throw invalid("allergens", "must not contain duplicates");
       215            if (!(body.get("optionGroups") instanceof List<?> rawGroups)) throw invalid("optionGroups", "must be an array");
       216            List<GroupInput> groups = new ArrayList<>();
       217            for (Object raw : rawGroups) {
       218                if (!(raw instanceof Map<?, ?> group)) throw invalid("optionGroups", "contains an invalid group");
       219                String groupName = text(group.get("name"), "optionGroups.name", 1, 80);
       220                String type = enumValue(group.get("selectionType"), "optionGroups.selectionType", List.of("SINGLE", "MULTIPLE"));
       221                boolean required = bool(group.get("required"), "optionGroups.required");
       222                int min = number(group.get("minSelections"), "optionGroups.minSelections").intValue(); int max = number(group.get("maxSelections"), "optionGroups.maxSelections").intValue();
       223                int order = number(group.get("displayOrder"), "optionGroups.displayOrder").intValue(); boolean active = bool(group.get("active"), "optionGroups.active");
       224                if (min < 0 || max < 1 || max > 20 || min > max || (required && min < 1) || ("SINGLE".equals(type) && max != 1)) throw invalid("optionGroups", "selection bounds are invalid");
       225                if (!(group.get("values") instanceof List<?> rawValues) || rawValues.isEmpty()) throw invalid("optionGroups.values", "must contain at least one value");
       226                List<ValueInput> values = new ArrayList<>();
       227                for (Object rawValue : rawValues) {
       228                    if (!(rawValue instanceof Map<?, ?> value)) throw invalid("optionGroups.values", "contains an invalid value");
       229                    values.add(new ValueInput(text(value.get("name"), "optionGroups.values.name", 1, 80), price(value.get("priceDelta"), "optionGroups.values.priceDelta"),
       230                            number(value.get("displayOrder"), "optionGroups.values.displayOrder").intValue(), bool(value.get("active"), "optionGroups.values.active")));
       231                }
       232                groups.add(new GroupInput(groupName, type, required, min, max, order, active, values));
       233            }
       234            return new DishInput(name, description, price, category, protein, spice, allergens, imageUrl, published, version, groups);
       235        }
       236
       237        private void saveChildren(long dishId, DishInput input) {
       238            jdbc.update("DELETE FROM dish_allergen WHERE dish_id=?", dishId);
       239            for (String allergen : input.allergens()) jdbc.update("INSERT INTO dish_allergen(dish_id,allergen) VALUES(?,?)", dishId, allergen);
       240            List<Long> oldGroups = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id FROM dish_option_group WHERE dish_id=?", Long.class, dishId);
       241            for (Long groupId : oldGroups) jdbc.update("DELETE FROM dish_option_value WHERE option_group_id=?", groupId);
       242            jdbc.update("DELETE FROM dish_option_group WHERE dish_id=?", dishId);
       243            for (GroupInput group : input.groups()) {
       244                jdbc.update("INSERT INTO dish_option_group(dish_id,name,selection_type,required_selection,min_selections,max_selections,display_order,active) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
       245                        dishId, group.name(), group.type(), group.required(), group.min(), group.max(), group.order(), group.active());
       246                long groupId = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT LAST_INSERT_ID()", Long.class);
       247                for (ValueInput value : group.values()) jdbc.update("INSERT INTO dish_option_value(option_group_id,name,price_delta,display_order,active) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)",
       248                        groupId, value.name(), value.delta(), value.order(), value.active());
       249            }
       250        }
       251
       252        private void ensureDishVersion(long id, long version) {
       253            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT version FROM dish WHERE id=?", id);
       254            if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "The dish was not found.");
       255            throw conflict("The dish has changed. Reload it before saving.");
       256        }
       257
       258        private Map<String, Object> getDailyMenu(LocalDate date) {
       259            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,status,publication_version FROM daily_menu WHERE menu_date=?", date);
       260            if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "The daily menu was not found.");
       261            Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0); long menuId = ((Number) row.get("id")).longValue();
       262            List<Map<String, Object>> items = jdbc.query("SELECT mi.id,mi.dish_id,d.name,mi.initial_stock,mi.remaining_stock,mi.visible,mi.stock_version FROM daily_menu_item mi JOIN dish d ON d.id=mi.dish_id WHERE mi.daily_menu_id=? ORDER BY mi.id", (rs, n) -> {
       263                Map<String, Object> item = new LinkedHashMap<>(); item.put("menuItemId", rs.getLong("id")); item.put("dishId", rs.getLong("dish_id")); item.put("dishName", rs.getString("name"));
       264                item.put("initialStock", rs.getInt("initial_stock")); item.put("remainingStock", rs.getInt("remaining_stock")); item.put("visible", rs.getBoolean("visible")); item.put("stockVersion", rs.getLong("stock_version")); return item;
       265            }, menuId);
       266            Map<String, Object> result = new LinkedHashMap<>(); result.put("date", date.toString()); result.put("status", row.get("status")); result.put("publicationVersion", ((Number) row.get("publication_version")).longValue()); result.put("items", items); return result;
       267        }
       268
       269        private void ensurePublishedDish(long id) { if (jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dish WHERE id=? AND published=TRUE", Integer.class, id) == 0) throw new ApiException(404, "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND", "A menu item must reference a published dish."); }
       270        private void bumpMenuVersions(long dishId) {
       271            jdbc.update("UPDATE daily_menu SET publication_version=publication_version+1 WHERE id IN (SELECT daily_menu_id FROM daily_menu_item WHERE dish_id=?)", dishId);
       272        }
       273        private String imageExtension(String contentType, byte[] bytes) {
       274            if (contentType == null) return null;
       275            if (contentType.equals("image/jpeg") && bytes.length > 3 && (bytes[0] & 255) == 255 && (bytes[1] & 255) == 216 && (bytes[2] & 255) == 255) return "jpg";
       276            if (contentType.equals("image/png") && bytes.length > 8 && bytes[0] == (byte) 0x89 && bytes[1] == 0x50 && bytes[2] == 0x4e && bytes[3] == 0x47) return "png";
       277            if (contentType.equals("image/gif") && bytes.length > 6 && bytes[0] == 'G' && bytes[1] == 'I' && bytes[2] == 'F') return "gif";
       278            if (contentType.equals("image/webp") && bytes.length > 12 && bytes[0] == 'R' && bytes[1] == 'I' && bytes[2] == 'F' && bytes[8] == 'W' && bytes[9] == 'E') return "webp";
       279            return null;
       280        }
       281        private Map<String, Object> pageMeta(int page, int size, long total) { Map<String, Object> r = new LinkedHashMap<>(); r.put("page", page); r.put("size", size); r.put("totalItems", total); r.put("totalPages", total == 0 ? 0 : (total + size - 1) / size); return r; }
       282        private void checkPage(int page, int size) { if (page < 0 || size < 1 || size > 100) throw invalid("page", "page must be non-negative and size must be between 1 and 100"); }
       283        private String text(Object value, String field, int min, int max) { if (!(value instanceof String s) || s.isBlank() || s.length() < min || s.length() > max) throw invalid(field, "must contain " + min + " to " + max + " characters"); return s; }
       284        private String enumValue(Object value, String field, List<String> allowed) { if (!(value instanceof String s) || !allowed.contains(s)) throw invalid(field, "contains an unsupported value"); return s; }
       285        private boolean bool(Object value, String field) { if (!(value instanceof Boolean b)) throw invalid(field, "must be a boolean"); return b; }
       286        private Number number(Object value, String field) { if (!(value instanceof Number n)) throw invalid(field, "must be a number"); return n; }
       287        private BigDecimal price(Object value, String field) { try { BigDecimal result = new BigDecimal(value.toString()); if (result.signum() < 0 || result.scale() > 2) throw invalid(field, "must be a non-negative money value with two decimals"); return result.setScale(2); } catch (RuntimeException ex) { if (ex instanceof ApiException ae) throw ae; throw invalid(field, "must be a money string"); } }
       288        private String money(BigDecimal value) { return value.setScale(2).toPlainString(); }
       289        private ApiException invalid(String field, String detail) { return new ApiException(400, "VALIDATION_FAILED", "The request contains invalid fields.", List.of(new ApiException.FieldError(field, detail))); }
       290        private ApiException conflict(String detail) { return new ApiException(409, "OPTIMISTIC_LOCK_CONFLICT", detail); }
       291        private record DishInput(String name, String description, BigDecimal price, String category, String protein, String spice, List<String> allergens, String imageUrl, boolean published, long version, List<GroupInput> groups) { }
       292        private record GroupInput(String name, String type, boolean required, int min, int max, int order, boolean active, List<ValueInput> values) { }
       293        private record ValueInput(String name, BigDecimal delta, int order, boolean active) { }
       294        private record MenuItemInput(long dishId, int initialStock, boolean visible) { }
       295    }
         1    package com.webox.platform.outbox;
         2
         3    import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
         4    import java.time.LocalDate;
         5    import java.util.List;
         6    import java.util.Map;
         7    import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
         8    import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
         9    import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
        10    import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
        11
        12    @Component
        13    public class OutboxPublisher {
        14        private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
        15        private final StringRedisTemplate redis;
        16        private final ObjectMapper mapper;
        17
        18        public OutboxPublisher(JdbcTemplate jdbc, StringRedisTemplate redis, ObjectMapper mapper) {
        19            this.jdbc = jdbc; this.redis = redis; this.mapper = mapper;
        20        }
        21
        22        @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "${webox.outbox.poll-delay-ms:500}")
        23        public void publishBatch() {
        24            List<Map<String, Object>> events = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT id,event_type,payload FROM outbox_event WHERE published_at IS NULL AND next_attempt_at<=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3) ORDER BY id LIMIT 50");
        25            for (Map<String, Object> event : events) publish(event);
        26        }
        27
        28        private void publish(Map<String, Object> event) {
        29            long id = ((Number) event.get("id")).longValue();
        30            try {
        31                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Map<String, Object> payload = mapper.readValue(event.get("payload").toString(), Map.class);
        32                String type = event.get("event_type").toString();
        33                if ("stock.changed".equals(type)) {
        34                    Object date = payload.get("date");
        35                    redis.convertAndSend("webox:stock:" + LocalDate.parse(date.toString()), mapper.writeValueAsString(payload));
        36                } else if ("order.status.changed".equals(type)) {
        37                    String date = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT delivery_date FROM customer_order WHERE id=?", String.class, Long.parseLong(event.get("aggregate_id").toString()));
        38                    if (date != null) redis.delete("webox:dashboard:" + date);
        39                }
        40                jdbc.update("UPDATE outbox_event SET published_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3) WHERE id=? AND published_at IS NULL", id);
        41            } catch (Exception ex) {
        42                jdbc.update("UPDATE outbox_event SET attempt_count=attempt_count+1,next_attempt_at=DATE_ADD(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(3),INTERVAL LEAST(attempt_count+1,30) SECOND) WHERE id=? AND published_at IS NULL", id);
        43            }
        44        }
        45    }
        45        public MenuController(JdbcTemplate jdbc, CurrentActor currentActor, RedisMessageListenerContainer listeners,
        46                              TaskScheduler scheduler, ObjectMapper mapper, StringRedisTemplate redis) {
        47            this.jdbc = jdbc; this.currentActor = currentActor; this.listeners = listeners; this.scheduler = scheduler; this.mapper = mapper; this.redis = redis;
        48        }
        49
        50        @GetMapping
        51        public Map<String, Object> menu(@RequestParam(required = false) LocalDate date,
        52                                        @RequestParam(required = false) String q,
        53                                        @RequestParam(required = false) List<String> categories,
        54                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "false") boolean recommended,
        55                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
        56                                        @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        57            LocalDate selectedDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")) : date;
        58            if (page < 0 || size < 1 || size > 100) throw invalid("page", "page must be non-negative and size must be between 1 and 100");
        59            if (q != null && q.length() > 50) throw invalid("q", "must be at most 50 characters");
        60            if (categories != null && categories.stream().anyMatch(c -> !List.of("CHINESE", "WESTERN", "JAPANESE", "LIGHT_MEAL", "KOREAN", "SOUTHEAST_ASIAN").contains(c)))
        61                throw invalid("categories", "contains an unsupported category");
        62            long userId = currentActor.require().userId();
        63            List<String> allergens = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM user_allergen WHERE user_id=?", String.class, userId);
        64            List<String> preferred = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT category FROM user_preferred_category WHERE user_id=?", String.class, userId);
        65            Map<String, Object> preference = preferences(userId);
        66            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = menuRows(selectedDate);
        67            if (q != null && !q.isBlank()) {
        68                String search = q.toLowerCase();
        69                rows.removeIf(row -> !row.get("name").toString().toLowerCase().contains(search)
        70                        && !row.get("description").toString().toLowerCase().contains(search));
        71            }
        72            if (categories != null && !categories.isEmpty()) rows.removeIf(row -> !categories.contains(row.get("category")));
        73            for (Map<String, Object> row : rows) {
        74                List<String> rowAllergens = jdbc.queryForList("SELECT allergen FROM dish_allergen WHERE dish_id=? ORDER BY allergen", String.class, row.get("dishId"));
        75                row.put("allergens", rowAllergens);
        76                List<String> matches = matches(row, preferred, preference);
        77                row.put("preferenceMatches", matches);
        78                row.put("allergenConflict", rowAllergens.stream().anyMatch(allergens::contains));
        79            }
        80            if (recommended) rows.removeIf(row -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(row.get("allergenConflict")));
        81            if (recommended) rows.sort(Comparator.<Map<String, Object>>comparingInt(row -> ((List<?>) row.get("preferenceMatches")).size()).reversed()
        82                    .thenComparing(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue()));
        83            else rows.sort(Comparator.comparing(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue()));
        84            long total = rows.size();
        85            int start = (int) Math.min(total, (long) page * size);
        86            int end = (int) Math.min(total, (long) start + size);
        87            List<Map<String, Object>> items = new ArrayList<>(rows.subList(start, end));
        88            for (Map<String, Object> item : items) item.remove("allergenConflict");
        89            Map<String, Object> result = pageMeta(page, size, total);
        90            result.put("date", selectedDate.toString()); result.put("items", items);
        91            return result;
        92        }

        96            LocalDate selectedDate = date == null ? LocalDate.now(ZoneId.of("Asia/Shanghai")) : date;
        97            long userId = currentActor.require().userId();
        98            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = menuRows(selectedDate).stream().filter(row -> ((Number) row.get("dishId")).longValue() == dishId).toList();

    @Configuration
    public class MediaResourceConfiguration implements WebMvcConfigurer {
        private final Path mediaRoot;
    
        public MediaResourceConfiguration(@Value("${webox.media.root:./uploads}") String mediaRoot) {
            this.mediaRoot = Path.of(mediaRoot).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        }
    
        @Override
        public void addResourceHandlers(ResourceHandlerRegistry registry) {
            String location = mediaRoot.toUri().toString();
            registry.addResourceHandler("/media/images/**").addResourceLocations(location.endsWith("/") ? location : location + "/");
        }
    }

    import type { ProblemDetails, TokenResponse } from './types';
    
    const API_BASE = '/api/v1';
    let accessToken: string | null = null;
    let refreshInFlight: Promise<boolean> | null = null;
    
    export class ProblemError extends Error {
      constructor(readonly problem: ProblemDetails) {
        super(problem.detail);
        this.name = 'ProblemError';
      }
    }
    
    export function setAccessToken(token: string): void {
      accessToken = token;
    }
    
    export function clearAccessToken(): void {
      accessToken = null;
    }
    
    export function getAccessToken(): string | null {
      return accessToken;
    }
    
    async function performRefresh(): Promise<boolean> {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/auth/refresh`, {
          method: 'POST',
          credentials: 'include',
        });
        if (!response.ok) {
          clearAccessToken();
          return false;
        }
        const tokenResponse = await response.json() as Partial<TokenResponse>;
        if (typeof tokenResponse.accessToken !== 'string' || !tokenResponse.accessToken) {
          clearAccessToken();
          return false;
        }
        setAccessToken(tokenResponse.accessToken);
        return true;
      } catch {
        clearAccessToken();
        return false;
      }
    }
    
    function refreshAccessToken(): Promise<boolean> {
      if (refreshInFlight) return refreshInFlight;
      const pending = performRefresh().finally(() => {
        if (refreshInFlight === pending) refreshInFlight = null;
      });
      refreshInFlight = pending;
      return pending;
    }
    
    export function bootstrapRefresh(): Promise<boolean> {
      return refreshAccessToken();
    }
    
    function isAuthSessionEndpoint(path: string): boolean {
      return /^\/auth\/(?:login|register|refresh|logout)(?:\?|$)/.test(path);
    }
    
    async function parseProblem(response: Response): Promise<ProblemError> {
      let body: Partial<ProblemDetails> | null = null;
      try {
        body = await response.json() as Partial<ProblemDetails>;
      } catch {
        body = null;
      }
      return new ProblemError({
        type: body?.type,
        title: body?.title,
        status: body?.status ?? response.status,
        detail: body?.detail ?? response.statusText ?? 'The request could not be completed.',
        code: body?.code ?? 'REQUEST_FAILED',
        correlationId: body?.correlationId,
        fieldErrors: body?.fieldErrors,
        details: body?.details,
      });
    }
    
    async function send<T>(path: string, init: RequestInit, token: string | null): Promise<T> {
      const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
      if (token) headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
      if (init.body && !(init.body instanceof FormData) && !headers.has('Content-Type')) {
        headers.set('Content-Type', 'application/json');
      }
      const requestInit = { ...init, headers };
      if (init.signal) {
        try {
          new Request('http://localhost/', { signal: init.signal });
        } catch {
          // Ignore signals from a different DOM runtime (for example, jsdom with Node fetch).
          requestInit.signal = undefined;
        }
      }
      const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path.startsWith('/') ? path : `/${path}`}`, {
        ...requestInit,
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw await parseProblem(response);
      if (response.status === 204 || response.headers.get('content-length') === '0') return undefined as T;
      return await response.json() as T;
    }
    
    export async function apiRequest<T>(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
      const originalToken = accessToken;
      try {
        return await send<T>(path, init, originalToken);
      } catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof ProblemError) || error.problem.status !== 401 || isAuthSessionEndpoint(path)) {
          throw error;
        }
        if (accessToken === originalToken && !(await refreshAccessToken())) throw error;
        return send<T>(path, init, accessToken);
      }
    }

    # Backend follow-up fixes report
    
    ## Implemented
    
    - Completed idempotency records are checked before live catalog planning, so same-key retries return the original order after dish publication changes. Processing claims are persisted in a short `REQUIRES_NEW` transaction. A concurrent same-key request gets `202` with `Retry-After: 1` when the claim row is locked; a same-key retry after a crash can reuse its reservation ID. MySQL confirmation remains transactional and `201` is returned only after commit.
    - Redis reservation Lua now stores reservation state and the reserved item quantities atomically with the decrement and Stream event. A repeated reservation ID with the same quantity spec does not decrement again. The Stream consumer group confirms reservations whose MySQL record is complete, retries pending entries after restart, and compensates failed or abandoned reservations once. Processing claims associated with an abandoned Stream event become terminal `FAILED` records after the configured grace period.
    - Admin daily-menu writes publish the committed MySQL remaining-stock values into Redis after commit and remove deleted item fields.
    - Recommended menu results no longer remove dishes matching saved allergens; allergen labels remain in results.
    - Redis fixed-window rate limits cover login by IP, order endpoints by authenticated user, and other API calls by authenticated user. Limits and windows are configurable. Rejections use `application/problem+json`, `RATE_LIMITED`, and `Retry-After`.
    - Placed orders add an `order.placed` outbox event that clears the dashboard cache. Seed media URLs under `/media/seed/**` now resolve beneath the configured media root, alongside `/media/images/**`.
    - Added contract checks for critical order, idempotency, money, `202`, and retry-header constraints, plus a live SSE stock-event integration test.
    
    ## Red-green evidence
    
    - Completed-key replay test returned `422` after the dish was unpublished; after moving the idempotency lookup ahead of planning, the retry returned the original order with `200`.
    - Recommended-menu regression failed because the shellfish dish was absent; it passes with that dish and its `SHELLFISH` label returned.
    - Admin stock-sync test observed stale Redis stock `2` after MySQL was edited to `8`; after-commit synchronization makes Redis report `8`.
    - Dashboard cache test found the old cache key after a placed order; publishing the new outbox event removes it and the subsequent snapshot includes the order.
    - Seed media test returned `404` for `/media/seed/image_01.jpg`; the mapped, configured seed directory now serves the exact bytes.
    - Login rate-limit test returned `401` for the third attempt before the filter existed; it now returns `429`, a `Retry-After` header, and `RATE_LIMITED`. Separate checks cover order and general API buckets.
    - A simulated MySQL insert failure after reservation initially left no durable failure row. The fix records `FAILED` after rollback and compensates stock; replay returns `409`.
    - A simulated crash with a durable `PROCESSING` claim and Redis reservation now resumes via the same idempotency key without a second Redis decrement. A stale processing Stream event is terminally compensated and acknowledged once; duplicate delivery does not restore twice.
    - Integration tests cover multi-item all-or-none reservation, stock-hash loss/rebuild from MySQL, completed replay after catalog change, dashboard invalidation using real order data, and live SSE delivery.
    
    ## Verification
    
    Command:
    
    ```text
    JAVA_HOME=/Library/Java/JavaVirtualMachines/jdk-17.0.12.jdk/Contents/Home ./mvnw verify
    ```
    
    Result: `BUILD SUCCESS`; 47 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped. The run used the `test` profile and `webox_test` with local Redis. MySQL 9.6 emitted Flyway's existing support-range warning (latest tested version 8.1); migrations validated and the suite passed.
    
    The test schema `webox_test` was recreated before the final full verification because repeated earlier runs had consumed seeded stock and accumulated invalid test-only user fixtures. No production schema or `webox` data was dropped. The final test fixtures use isolated future menu dates and BCrypt password hashes.
    
    ## Residual concerns
    
    - Confirmation remains synchronous. `202` is used for a competing request that sees an active processing lock; normal requests return `201` after the transaction commits. A same-key POST retries a durable processing claim after a crash. The Stream worker does not reconstruct and automatically rerun the complete order request: after the grace period it marks an unconfirmed Stream-backed claim failed and compensates it. A crash after the durable claim but before Redis appends a Stream event is resumed by a repeated POST; without that retry, no Stream entry exists for the recovery worker to inspect.
    - Redis inventory rebuild is lazy per item from MySQL on reservation. The tests cover complete inventory-hash loss and admin edit synchronization; there is not yet a periodic whole-date drift reconciler for partial Redis corruption.
    - OpenAPI behavior/schema checks read the binding YAML, but request bodies are still manually parsed rather than validated by a full runtime OpenAPI validator. Shared OpenAPI and design documents were not edited per ownership constraints.
    
    No frontend files, shared docs, copied media assets, or commits were changed.
    # WeBox Frontend Handoff
    
    Status: **DONE_WITH_CONCERNS**
    
    ## Delivered
    
    - Preserved and extended the existing typed API client, memory-only access token, refresh single-flight behavior, authentication pages, role guards, menu, cart, preferences, and idempotency helper.
    - Added employee checkout with authoritative quotes, slot acknowledgement, address selection/inline address, budget warning, UUID idempotency, asynchronous `202` polling, success-only cart clearing, order history/detail, and Pending cancellation.
    - Added employee/Admin route trees and responsive layouts. The Admin Console includes paged catalog search/filter, dish create/edit/publication/image upload, nested option validation, daily menu stock/visibility/publishing, order detail and allowed versioned transitions, and live/manual-refresh dashboard metrics with ECharts and an accessible trend table.
    - Added bearer-authenticated stock SSE parsing, version ordering, reconnect backoff, focus/interval revalidation, and sold-out control updates.
    - Added MSW component/unit coverage, desktop/mobile Playwright scenarios, and axe checks for login, employee menu, and Admin dashboard.
    - Updated ECharts to 6.1.0, Zod to 4.6.7, and Vitest to 4.1.12. `npm audit` reports zero vulnerabilities.
    
    ## Red → Green Evidence
    
    - Initial takeover baseline: `npm test -- --run` had 25 passing tests and one failed checkout suite because `CheckoutPage` did not exist. `npm run type-check` also reported the same absent checkout module, an unused checkout test import, and Money/test response typing errors. `npm run lint` could not find `e2e/`; build stopped on the same TypeScript errors.
    - Checkout: the absent-page failure was retained as the initial red. Added checkout behavior and a bearer/idempotency assertion; the focused checkout suite then passed (3 tests), including adjusted-slot acknowledgement, `202` polling, and bearer header/key checks.
    - Orders: `src/features/orders/orders.test.tsx` first failed resolving the absent detail/list modules. After implementation, the suite passed (2 tests) for paged links and versioned Pending cancellation.
    - Admin catalog: the suite first failed resolving `CatalogPage`. A subsequent option-limit case reached POST before validation and failed its expected alert; adding the nested constraints made all 3 catalog tests pass, including publication with `expectedVersion`.
    - Daily menu, Admin order queue/detail, dashboard, and app routes each first failed on their absent module; their focused suites then passed (1, 1, 1, 1, and 1 test respectively).
    - SSE: `src/api/sse.test.ts` first failed resolving the absent module. The first stream transport test exposed a jsdom/Node `AbortSignal` realm mismatch; compatible-signal handling made all 3 SSE tests pass.
    - Responsive E2E: the first mobile Admin run timed out because inactive sidebar entries were hidden. Showing the icon navigation at mobile widths made the focused mobile test pass.
    - Accessibility: axe initially reported text contrast, the dish button name mismatch, and a missing role on the dashboard chart placeholder. Correcting styles and accessible names/roles made the axe scans pass.
    - Nested option limits: the added `minSelections <= maxSelections` test failed by sending an invalid dish POST and showing the generic server error; validating the nested option schema made it pass.
    
    ## Final Verification
    
    All commands were run from `frontend/` on 2026-10-24.
    
    | Command | Result |
    |---|---|
    | `npm run lint` | Passed, exit 0 |
    | `npm run type-check` | Passed, exit 0 |
    | `npm test -- --run` | 18 test files passed; 41 tests passed |
    | `npm run build` | Passed, 801 modules transformed; largest split chunks: ECharts 342.64 kB and ZRender 184.19 kB; no chunk-size warning |
    | `npm run test:e2e` | 6 passed across Chromium desktop and mobile projects, including axe scans |
    | `npm audit` | 0 vulnerabilities |
    
    ## Concerns / Limits
    
    - Playwright used route mocks; a live backend integration run remains for coordinator verification.
    - The Admin dish editor exposes nested option groups as validated JSON rather than a guided group/value builder. It can create and edit contract-compliant data, but is less approachable for day-to-day catalog editing.
    - The screens use semantic native controls with custom CSS. Ant Design remains installed but is not currently used by the page components.
    - Vite emits non-failing warnings that it removed misplaced pure annotations from Zod dependency comments. Vitest also emits the environment warning `--localstorage-file was provided without a valid path`; all commands exit successfully.
    
    No files under `backend/` or `docs/` were changed. No commit was created.

    package com.webox.identity.infrastructure;
    
    import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
    import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
    import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
    import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
    import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
    
    @Component
    @Profile("local")
    public class DemoUserBootstrap implements org.springframework.boot.ApplicationRunner {
        private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;
        private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
        private final String adminEmail;
        private final String adminPassword;
        private final String employeeEmail;
        private final String employeePassword;
    
        public DemoUserBootstrap(JdbcTemplate jdbc, PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder,
                                 @Value("${webox.demo.admin-email:admin@webox.local}") String adminEmail,
                                 @Value("${WEBOX_DEMO_ADMIN_PASSWORD:}") String adminPassword,
                                 @Value("${webox.demo.employee-email:employee@webox.local}") String employeeEmail,
                                 @Value("${WEBOX_DEMO_EMPLOYEE_PASSWORD:}") String employeePassword) {
            this.jdbc = jdbc; this.passwordEncoder = passwordEncoder; this.adminEmail = adminEmail;
            this.adminPassword = adminPassword; this.employeeEmail = employeeEmail; this.employeePassword = employeePassword;
        }
    
        @Override
        public void run(org.springframework.boot.ApplicationArguments args) {
            createIfConfigured(adminEmail, adminPassword, "WeBox Admin", "ADMIN");
            createIfConfigured(employeeEmail, employeePassword, "WeBox Employee", "EMPLOYEE");
        }
    
        private void createIfConfigured(String email, String password, String displayName, String role) {
            if (password == null || password.isBlank()) return;
            if (jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM app_user WHERE email=?", Integer.class, email) > 0) return;
            jdbc.update("INSERT INTO app_user(email,password_hash,display_name,role,enabled) VALUES(?,?,?,?,TRUE)",
                    email.trim().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT), passwordEncoder.encode(password), displayName, role);
        }
    }

    # WeBox
    
    WeBox is an enterprise employee meal-ordering platform with a responsive employee application and an Admin Console. It implements the complete Tier 1 and Tier 2 scope from the supplied PRD, plus real-time inventory deduction and the operations dashboard from Tier 3. The AI recommendation boundary is reserved for a future provider integration.
    
    All user-facing content, validation feedback, menu data, and dashboard labels are English.
    
    ## Features
    
    ### Employee application
    
    - Email registration and login with BCrypt passwords
    - Short-lived access JWT plus rotating `HttpOnly` refresh cookie
    - Daily menu search, multi-category filters, dish details, options, and exact CNY pricing
    - Dietary preferences, cuisine/spice ranking, allergen confirmation, and budget warnings
    - Configuration-aware cart with a five-serving order limit
    - Server-authoritative quote, cutoff-slot adjustment, idempotent placement, and inventory validation
    - Order history, details, and Pending-order cancellation
    - Live stock updates over authenticated SSE, including low-stock and sold-out states
    
    ### Admin Console
    
    - Dish search, creation, editing, publication, option groups, allergens, and image uploads
    - Daily menu and stock management for today or future dates
    - Order search, details, audited status transitions, and cancellation
    - Dashboard overview, Top 10 dishes, meal-slot distribution, seven-day trend, and low-stock alerts
    
    ## Architecture
    
    ```text
    React + TypeScript SPA
            │ JSON / SSE
            │ Bearer JWT + refresh cookie
            ▼
    Java 17 + Spring Boot modular backend
            ├── MySQL: durable users, catalog, menus, orders, stock and reporting
            └── Redis: refresh state, caches, rate limits, inventory reservations,
                       Streams, Pub/Sub and SSE fan-out
    ```
    
    - Employee APIs: `/api/v1/app/**`
    - Admin APIs: `/api/v1/admin/**`
    - Authentication APIs: `/api/v1/auth/**`
    - Binding contract: [`docs/openapi.yaml`](docs/openapi.yaml)
    - Architecture details: [`docs/architecture.md`](docs/architecture.md)
    
    Inventory admission uses an all-or-none Redis Lua reservation and Redis Stream record. MySQL conditionally confirms stock before an order can return success. Reservation IDs are idempotent; pending Stream entries support recovery and one-time compensation. MySQL remains the reporting and confirmed-inventory authority.
    
    ## Technology stack
    
    | Area | Technology |
    |---|---|
    | Frontend | React 19, TypeScript, Vite, React Router, TanStack Query, Zustand, React Hook Form, Zod, ECharts |
    | Backend | Java 17, Spring Boot 3.3, Spring Security, Spring JDBC, Flyway, springdoc-openapi |
    | Data | MySQL, Redis |
    | Testing | JUnit 5, MockMvc, ArchUnit, Vitest, Testing Library, MSW, Playwright, axe |
    
    ## Repository layout
    
    ```text
    backend/                  Spring Boot service, migrations and backend tests
    frontend/                 React SPA, unit tests and browser tests
    docs/                     architecture, API, schema and development documentation
    backend/uploads/seed/     local seed dish images
    ```
    
    ## Prerequisites
    
    - JDK 17
    - Maven 3.9+ or the included Maven Wrapper
    - Node.js and npm
    - MySQL reachable at `127.0.0.1:3306`
    - Redis reachable at `127.0.0.1:6379`
    
    The local defaults use MySQL user `root` with an empty password and Redis without authentication. Do not use these credentials outside local development.
    
    ## Local setup
    
    ### 1. Create the database
    
    ```bash
    mysql -h 127.0.0.1 -P 3306 -u root -e \
      "CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS webox CHARACTER SET utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci;"
    redis-cli -h 127.0.0.1 -p 6379 ping
    ```
    
    Flyway applies the schema and English menu seed automatically when the backend starts.
    
    ### 2. Start the backend
    
    ```bash
    cd backend
    
    export WEBOX_DB_URL='jdbc:mysql://127.0.0.1:3306/webox?useUnicode=true&characterEncoding=utf8&serverTimezone=Asia/Shanghai'
    export WEBOX_DB_USER='root'
    export WEBOX_DB_PASSWORD=''
    export WEBOX_REDIS_HOST='127.0.0.1'
    export WEBOX_REDIS_PORT='6379'
    export WEBOX_JWT_SECRET='replace-this-with-a-random-secret-of-at-least-32-bytes'
    export WEBOX_DEMO_ADMIN_PASSWORD='Admin1234'
    export WEBOX_DEMO_EMPLOYEE_PASSWORD='Employee1234'
    
    JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17) ./mvnw spring-boot:run
    ```
    
    On non-macOS systems, set `JAVA_HOME` to the installed JDK 17 directory instead.
    
    Optional local accounts created by these environment variables:
    
    | Role | Email | Password source |
    |---|---|---|
    | Admin | `admin@webox.local` | `WEBOX_DEMO_ADMIN_PASSWORD` |
    | Employee | `employee@webox.local` | `WEBOX_DEMO_EMPLOYEE_PASSWORD` |
    
    The bootstrap is idempotent and stores only BCrypt hashes.
    
    Backend URLs:
    
    - API: `http://localhost:8080`
    - Health: `http://localhost:8080/actuator/health`
    - OpenAPI: `http://localhost:8080/v3/api-docs`
    - Swagger UI: `http://localhost:8080/swagger-ui/index.html`
    
    ### 3. Start the frontend
    
    In another terminal:
    
    ```bash
    cd frontend
    npm ci
    npm run dev
    ```
    
    Open `http://localhost:5173`. Vite proxies `/api` to the backend at `http://localhost:8080`.
    
    ## Verification
    
    Backend tests use the dedicated `webox_test` schema and the local Redis instance. Never point the test profile at production data.
    
    ```bash
    cd backend
    JAVA_HOME=$(/usr/libexec/java_home -v 17) ./mvnw verify
    ```
    
    Latest backend verification: **47 tests, 0 failures, 0 errors, 0 skipped**.
    
    ```bash
    cd frontend
    npm ci
    npm run lint
    npm run type-check
    npm test -- --run
    npm run build
    npm run test:e2e
    npm audit
    ```
    
    Latest frontend verification: **41 unit tests and 6 desktop/mobile Playwright + axe checks passed**, with zero npm audit vulnerabilities. Browser E2E currently uses API route mocks; a live full-stack E2E run remains a recommended final deployment check.
    
    ## Important design rules
    
    - Money is transferred as two-decimal strings, calculated with `BigDecimal` on the backend, and handled as integer fen on the frontend.
    - The backend always recalculates prices and validates dish options, availability, cutoff rules, quantity limits, and ownership.
    - A Redis reservation is not by itself a successful order; success requires a committed MySQL order.
    - Employees can cancel only `Pending` orders. Admin transitions are `Pending → Confirmed → Completed`, with cancellation allowed from `Pending` or `Confirmed`.
    - Dashboard values come from real orders. Cancelled orders count toward total orders but not revenue or rankings.
    - Allergens do not hide dishes from the menu. The employee receives a confirmation warning when adding a matching dish.
    
    ## Documentation
    
    - [Requirements and scope](docs/requirements-and-scope.md)
    - [Frontend design](docs/frontend-design.md)
    - [Backend design](docs/backend-design.md)
    - [API guide](docs/api.md)
    - [MySQL schema](docs/database/schema.sql)
    - [Development plan](docs/development-plan.md)
    - [Appendix A score assessment](docs/appendix-a-score-assessment.md)
    
    ## Known limitations
    
    - The AI recommendation provider is intentionally not implemented; the module/API extension seam is reserved.
    - Recovery resumes a durable `Processing` submission when the client retries the same idempotency key. A stale unconfirmed Stream reservation is compensated and marked failed rather than reconstructing the full order automatically.
    - Redis inventory rebuild is lazy per item; there is no scheduled full-date drift scan.
    - Several supplied seed images are approximate visual matches because the asset package does not contain an exact photograph for every PRD dish.
    - Before final candidate delivery, place the complete original AI coding conversation exports under `ai-conversations/` as required by the PRD.
